<script>
    (function () {
        'use strict';

        window.common = {
            isUndefined: isUndefined,
            isDefined: isDefined,
            isUndefinedOrNull: isUndefinedOrNull
        };

        function isUndefined(obj) {
            return typeof (obj) === typeof (undefined);
        }

        function isDefined(obj) {
            return typeof (obj) !== typeof (undefined);
        }

        function isUndefinedOrNull(obj) {
            return typeof (obj) === typeof (undefined) || obj === null;
        }

    })();
</script>
<script>
    var EventManager = function () {

        var eventTypes = {
            favoriteItemAdded: 'favoriteItemAdded',
            favoriteItemRemoved: 'favoriteItemRemoved',
            cartItemAdded: 'cartItemAdded',
            cartItemUpdated: 'cartItemUpdated',
            cartItemRemoved: 'cartItemRemoved',
            hashtagsLoaded: 'hashtagsLoaded',
            resultsLoaded: 'results_loaded',
            experimentsLoaded: 'experiments_loaded'
        };

        this.raiseEvent = raiseEvent;
        this.eventTypes = eventTypes;

        function raiseEvent(eventType, data) {

            //Logger.debug('Event: ' + eventType, data);

            $(window).trigger(eventType, data);
        }
    };
</script>
<script>
    var CartManager = function (eventManager) {

        if (!eventManager)
            eventManager = new EventManager();

        var cartActions = {
            add: 1,
            update: 2,
            remove: 3
        };

        this.validateBeforeAdd = validateBeforeAdd;
        this.updateCart = updateCart;
        this.submitCartUpdate = submitCartUpdate;
        this.cartActions = cartActions;

        function validateBeforeAdd(stockCount, cartCount, addQuantity) {
            if (stockCount >= cartCount + addQuantity)
                return true;

            return false;
        }

        function updateCart(sid, action, quantity) {
            var url;
            var eventType;

            switch (action) {
                case cartActions.add:
                    url = "/cart/addItem?sid=" + sid + "&qu=" + quantity;
                    eventType = eventManager.eventTypes.cartItemAdded;
                    break;
                case cartActions.update:
                    url = "/cart/setItemQuantity?sid=" + sid + "&qu=" + quantity;
                    eventType = eventManager.eventTypes.cartItemUpdated;
                    break;
                case cartActions.remove:
                    url = "/cart/removeItem?sid=" + sid;
                    eventType = eventManager.eventTypes.cartItemRemoved;
                default:
                    break;
            }

            return $.ajax({
                url: url,
                cache: false,
                complete: function () {
                    eventManager.raiseEvent(eventType, { itemId: sid });
                }
            });
        }

        function submitCartUpdate(title) {
            var url = '/cart?addedItemTitle=' + title;
            window.location.href = url;
        }
    };
</script>
<script>
    var Marks = function (createCookieFunc, eraseCookieFunc, eventManager) {

        var createCookie = createCookieFunc;
        var eraseCookie = eraseCookieFunc;

        this.markMeDesk = markMeDesk;
        this.addMark = addMark;
        this.confirmUnmark = confirmUnmark;
        this.undoRemoveMark = undoRemoveMark;
        this.getCurrentMarks = getCurrentMarks;
        this.removeMark = removeMark;
        this.isMarked = isMarked;

        this.wishlistUpdated = false;

        $(document).ready(function () {
            refreshMarksCount();
            initFavoritesPageMarkButtons();
        });

        function markMeDesk(thisimgelement, event, uid, markorcart) {
            if (event.preventDefault) {
                event.preventDefault();
            }
            else
                event.returnValue = false;

            var st = ($(thisimgelement).attr('src'));
            var isMarked = st.indexOf("_RO") > 1;
            var oldMarks = getCurrentMarks();

            if (isMarked) {
                if (markorcart == "mark") {
                    oldMarks = removeMark(uid, oldMarks);
                }
                else {
                    if (uid.length > 0) {
                        updateCart(uid, 3, -1);
                    }
                }
            }
            else {
                if (markorcart == "mark") {
                    addMark(uid, oldMarks);
                }
                else {
                    if (uid.length > 0) {
                        updateCart(uid, 1, 1);
                        raiseEvent(eventManager.eventTypes.cartItemAdded, uid);
                    }
                }
            }

            updateMyMarkImage(thisimgelement, isMarked);

            updateCountIndicator(markorcart, oldMarks.length);

            wishlistUpdated = true;
        }

        function addMark(uid, currentMarks) {
            var marks = currentMarks;
            if (!currentMarks)
                marks = getCurrentMarks();

            marks.push(uid);

            eraseCookie("Marks");
            createCookie("Marks", marks.join('$'));

            updateCountIndicator('mark', marks.length);

            raiseEvent(eventManager.eventTypes.favoriteItemAdded, uid);
        }

        function raiseEvent(eventName, itemId) {
            eventManager.raiseEvent(eventName, { itemId: itemId });
        }

        function confirmUnmark(element, event) {
            event.preventDefault();
            var favoriteItem = $(element).closest('.favorite-item');
            favoriteItem.addClass('unmark-confirmation');

            disableLink(favoriteItem);

            var currentMarks = getCurrentMarks();
            removeMark(favoriteItem.data('uid'), currentMarks);

            wishlistUpdated = true;
        }

        function undoRemoveMark(element, event) {
            event.preventDefault();
            var favoriteItem = $(element).closest('.favorite-item');

            enableLink(favoriteItem);

            var currentMarks = getCurrentMarks();
            var uid = favoriteItem.data('uid');
            currentMarks.push(uid);

            eraseCookie("Marks");
            createCookie("Marks", currentMarks.join('$'));

            favoriteItem.removeClass('unmark-confirmation');

            raiseEvent(eventManager.eventTypes.favoriteItemAdded, uid);

            updateCountIndicator('mark', currentMarks.length);

            wishlistUpdated = true;
        }

        function refreshMarksCount() {
            var marks = getCurrentMarks();
            updateCountIndicator('mark', marks.length);
        }

        function getCurrentMarks() {
            var currentMarksCookie = readCookie('Marks');
            var marksArray = (currentMarksCookie !== null && currentMarksCookie !== '') ? currentMarksCookie.split('$') : [];
            return marksArray;
        }

        function removeMark(markId, currentMarks) {
            var marks = currentMarks;
            if (!currentMarks)
                marks = getCurrentMarks();

            var currentMarkIndex = marks.indexOf(markId);
            if (currentMarkIndex >= 0) {
                marks.splice(currentMarkIndex, 1);

                var newMarks = marks.join('$');

                eraseCookie("Marks");
                createCookie("Marks", newMarks);

                updateCountIndicator('mark', marks.length);

                raiseEvent(eventManager.eventTypes.favoriteItemRemoved, markId);
            }

            return marks;
        }

        function isMarked(itemId) {
            var currentMarks = getCurrentMarks();
            return currentMarks.indexOf(itemId) >= 0;
        }

        function updateMyMarkImage(thisimgelement, isMarked) {
            if (thisimgelement === null)
                return;
            var parent = $(thisimgelement).parent();

            $(thisimgelement).attr("data-ismarked", !isMarked);
            var imgsrclink = thisimgelement != null ? ($(thisimgelement).attr('src')) : null;

            if ($(thisimgelement).parents(".galleryResContainer").length > 0) {
                var fileNameExtension = imgsrclink.substring(imgsrclink.length - 4, imgsrclink.length);

                if (isMarked) {
                    parent.find(".mymarkIco").toggleClass('active');
                    parent.find(".mymarkIco").removeAttr('style');
                } else {
                    pulse(parent.find(".mymarkIco.selected"));
                    parent.find(".mymarkIco").toggleClass('active');
                    parent.find(".mymarkIco").removeAttr('style');
                }
            }
        }

        function pulse(imga) {
            $(imga).animate({
                width: 34, height: 30,
                opacity: 0.8
            }, 10, function () {
                $(imga).animate({
                    width: 30, height: 26,
                    opacity: 1
                }), 10
            })
        }

        function updateCountIndicator(counterName, itemsCount) {
            $("#my" + counterName + "spicn").attr("num", itemsCount);

            $("#" + counterName + "indicator").html(itemsCount);
            if (itemsCount > 0) {
                $("#not" + counterName + "edmsg").css('display', 'none');
                $('#markindicator').css('display', 'inline');
            }
            else {
                $("#not" + counterName + "edmsg").css('display', 'block');
                $('#markindicator').css('display', 'none');
            }
        }

        function initFavoritesPageMarkButtons() {
            var fadedCssClass = 'faded';

            var buttons = $('.favorite-item .mark-toggle');
            buttons.on('mouseenter', function () {
                var item = $(this).closest('.favorite-item');
                item.addClass(fadedCssClass);
            });

            buttons.on('mouseout', function () {
                var item = $(this).closest('.favorite-item');
                item.removeClass(fadedCssClass);
            });
        }

        function disableLink(element) {
            var elem = $(element);
            var originalHref = elem.attr('href');
            elem.removeAttr('href');
            elem.attr('orig-href', originalHref);
        }

        function enableLink(element) {
            var elem = $(element);
            elem.attr('href', elem.attr('orig-href'));
            elem.removeAttr('orig-href');
        }
    };
</script>
<script>
    function fieldIsEmpty(str) {
        if ((str == "") || (str == null))
            return true;
        return !str.replace(/^\s+/g, '').length; // boolean ('true' if field is empty)
    }

    function checkEmail(str) {
        var re = /^([\w-]+(?:\.[\w-]+)*)\@((?:[\w-]+\.)*\w[\w-]{0,66})\.([a-z]{2,6}(?:\.[a-z]{2})?)$/i;
        return re.test(str);
    }

    function checkPassword(str) {
        var re = /^(?=.*[a-zA-Z])(?=.*\d)[A-Za-z\d$@$!%*?&]{4,}/; // At least one number, one lowercase and one uppercase letter, at least four characters
        return ((re.test(str)) && (!(fieldIsEmpty(str))));
    }

    function validatePassword(elem) {
        value = $(elem).val();
        var errorElem = $(elem).parent().find(".validation_message").first();
        var isValid = true;

        if (!checkPassword(value)) {
            isValid = false;
            $(errorElem).html("* Password should include at least 4 characters, including one letter and one number.");
            $(elem).addClass("input_err");
        }
        else {
            $(errorElem).text("");
            $(elem).removeClass("input_err");
        }
        return isValid;
    }

    function validateEmail(elem) {
        value = $(elem).val();
        var errorElem = $(elem).parent().find(".validation_message").first();
        var isValid = true;
        if (fieldIsEmpty(value)) {
            isValid = false;
            $(errorElem).text("* Required.");
            $(elem).addClass("input_err");
        }
        else if (!checkEmail(value)) {
            isValid = false;
            $(errorElem).text("* Please enter a valid email address.");
            $(elem).addClass("input_err");
        }
        else {
            $(errorElem).text("");
            $(elem).removeClass("input_err");
        }
        return isValid;
    }

    function validateConfirm(elem) {
        value = $(elem).val();
        var errorElem = $(elem).parent().find(".validation_message").first();
        var isValid = true;
        var targetId = $(elem).data("target");

        if (value != $(targetId).val()) {
            isValid = false;
            $(errorElem).text("* Fields do not match.");
            $(elem).addClass("input_err");
        }
        else {
            $(errorElem).text("");
            $(elem).removeClass("input_err");
        }
        return isValid;
    }

    function validateRequired(elem) {
        value = $(elem).val();
        var errorElem = $(elem).parent().find(".validation_message").first();
        var isValid = !fieldIsEmpty(value);
        if (!isValid) {
            $(errorElem).text("* Required.");
            $(elem).addClass("input_err");
        }
        else {
            $(errorElem).text("");
            $(elem).removeClass("input_err");
        }
        return isValid;
    }
</script>

    function startSliding() {
        setInterval(function () {
            if (!window.isOnSlide) {
                if (window.slideposGen == grabWidgetCount - 1) {
                    slide(0);
                }
                else {
                    slide(parseInt(window.slideposGen, 10) + 1);
                }
            }
        }, 3000);
    }
    function slide(newplace, speed) {
        $('#itemSlide' + slideposGen).fadeOut(speed, function () {
            unmarkobj($('.dot[place=' + slideposGen + ']'));
            $('#itemSlide' + (newplace)).fadeIn(speed);
            markobj($('.dot[place=' + newplace + ']'));
            window.slideposGen = newplace;
        });
    }

    function unmarkobj(ssc) {
        var st = ($(ssc).attr('src'));
        if (st !== undefined) {
            $(ssc).attr('src', st.replace("_RO.png", ".png"));
        }
    }
    function markobj(ssc) {
        var st = ($(ssc).attr('src'));
        if (st !== undefined) {

    }

    $('#sbtb').autocomplete({
        source: function (req, add) {
            if (!launched)
                return;
            req.term = req.term.trim();
            $.ajax({
                type: "GET",
                cache: false,
                url: "/ss?q=" + encodeURIComponent(req.term).replace(/\+/g, '%2B'),
                success: function (data) {
                    if (!launched)
                        return;
                    var array = [];
                    for (var x = 0; x < data.suggestions.length; x++) {
                        var i = data.suggestions[x];
                        array.push({
                            value: i.replace(/Bbb_Bd/g, "").replace(/Bbb_Bc/g, "").replace(/&#39;/, "'").replace("&amp;", "&"),
                            title: i.replace(/Bbb_Bd/g, "<b>").replace(/Bbb_Bc/g, "</b>")
                        });
                    }
                    add(array);
                }
            });
        },

        open: function (e, ui) {

        },

        select: function (event, ui) {
            $(".loadingDiv").show();
            var sf = "";
            if ($("#category_filter").attr("stcId") != null)
                sf = "&stcId=*" + $("#category_filter").attr("stcId");
            var tr = "search_page";
            if ($('.searchButton').first().attr('ishomepage') == 1) {
                tr = "homepage_search";
            }
            document.location = "/s/" + encodeURI(ui.item.value).replace(/\+/g, '%2B') + "?tr=" + tr + sf; // .replace(/%20/g, '+')
            $("#sbtb").autocomplete("close");

            launched = false;
        },

        messages: {
            noResults: '',
            results: function () { }
        }
    });

    function submitSearch(input) {
        launched = false;
        var v = input.val();
        var sf = "";
        if ($("#category_filter").attr("stcId") != null)
            sf = "&stcId=*" + $("#category_filter").attr("stcId");
        v = v.trim();

        if (v == "")
            return false;

        v = v.replace(/[&\/\\#,~'":*?<>{}]/g, ''); // Remove special characters: & / \ # , ~  ' " : * ? < > { }

        var tr = "search_page";
        var page = "Search";
        if ($('.searchButton').first().attr('ishomepage') == 1) {
            tr = "homepage_search";
            page = "Home";
        }
        //trackSiteDataFromJs('SearchField_Enter');
        document.location = "/s/" + encodeURIComponent(v).replace(/%2B/g, '+').replace(/%20/g, '+') + "?tr=" + tr + sf;
        $("#sbtb").autocomplete("close");
    }

    function addStringAtPosition(str, strAtPos, fromPointIndexOf, afterPlaceOString, stringToAdd) {
        return str.substring(0, str.indexOf(strAtPos, fromPointIndexOf) + afterPlaceOString) + stringToAdd + str.substring(str.indexOf(strAtPos, fromPointIndexOf) + afterPlaceOString, str.length);
    }

    function removeParam(inputurl, parToRemove) {
        if (inputurl.indexOf(parToRemove) > 0) {
            var placeEndofparam = inputurl.indexOf('&', inputurl.indexOf(parToRemove) + 1);
            var intislastword = 0;
            if (placeEndofparam < 1) {
                placeEndofparam = inputurl.length - 1;
                intislastword = 1;
            }
            inputurl = inputurl.replace(inputurl.substring(inputurl.indexOf(parToRemove) - intislastword, placeEndofparam + 1), '');
        }
        return inputurl;
    }

    function removeHoverFromTablets() {

    function getAdFromAdServer(adObj, url, adPos, div) {
        $.ajax({
            type: "POST",
            url: url,
            contentType: "application/json",
            data: JSON.stringify(adObj),
        }).done(function (data, textStatus, jqXHR) {
            if (jqXHR.status !== 200) {
                $(adPos).remove();
                return;
            }
            $(adPos).html(data);
        });
    }


    $(document).on('scroll', function () { // Load more items when scrolling search results page
        if ($("#morebuttonContainer").length && $("#morebuttonContainer").position().top > 0
            && $(window).scrollTop() > ($(".resultsCon .reItem").last().offset().top - 1300)
            && !window.isLoaded && $('.galleryResContainer').length > 4) {
            window.isLoaded = true;
            loadMoreItems();
        }

        if ($(window).scrollTop() >  72) {
            $("#chmas-banner").slideUp(500);
        }

        if ($(window).scrollTop() <= 72) {
            $("#chmas-banner").slideDown(500);
        }

    });

    function loadMoreItems() {
        if ($('.loadedItems').length > 0)
            return;
        try {
            $("#morebuttonContainer").hide();
            $('#loadmoreContainer').show();
            window.paging++;
            window.isLoaded = true;
            
            var url = "/s/" + window.term + "?page=" + window.paging;

            $.ajax({
                method : "POST",
                data: { filters: JSON.stringify(filtersForRequest) },
                url: url
            }).done(function (html) {
                if ($.trim(html).length) {
                    var cartDiv = document.createElement('div');
                    cartDiv.id = "newItems" + window.paging;
                    cartDiv.className = "loadedItems";
                    cartDiv.innerHTML = html;
                    cartDiv.style.display = "none";
                    if (html.indexOf("galleryResContainer") > 0) {
                        $("#resultscontainer").append($(cartDiv));
                        $('#loadmoreContainer').hide();
                        $("#morebuttonContainer").show();
                        removeHoverFromTablets();
                    }
                    else { // No more results
                        $('#loadmoreContainer').hide();
                        $("#morebuttonContainer").hide();
                    }
                }
                else {
                    $('#loadmoreContainer').hide();
                    $("#morebuttonContainer").hide();
                }
                window.isWaiting = false;
            }).fail(function (er) {
                $('div#loadmoreContainer').hide();
                //trackSiteDataFromJs('end_showing_more_error');
            }).always();
        }
        catch (err) {
            //Logger.debug(err);
        }
    }

    function showMoreItemsShow() {
        $("#morebuttonContainer").hide();
        var st = document.getElementById("newItems" + window.paging);
        if (st != null) {
            displaynextItems(st);
        }
        else {
            $('div#loadmoreContainer').show();            
            setTimeout(function () {
                var st = document.getElementById("newItems" + window.paging);
                if (st != null) {
                    displaynextItems(st);
                }
                else {
                    setTimeout(function () {
                        st = document.getElementById("newItems" + window.paging);
                        if (st != null) {
                            displaynextItems(st);
                        }
                        else {
                            //$(document).remove("#morebuttonContainer");
                            $('div#loadmoreContainer').hide();
                            $("#morebuttonContainer").hide();
                            return;
                        }
                    }, 6000);
                }
            }, 3000);
        }
        window.isLoaded = false;;
    }

    function displaynextItems(st) {
        //$("#morebuttonContainer").show();
        window.isLoaded = false;
        window.btmreached = false;
        var items = $('#' + st.id + ' .resultsCon').children();

        $(items).insertAfter($(".resultsCon").first().find('.reItem').last());
        $('#' + st.id).remove();
        $('div#loadmoreContainer').hide();
        
        ArrowBackToTopShowAndHide();
    }

    function checknumItemtoshowMoreBtn() {
        try {
            var numitem = getItemsOnPage();
            if (numitem > 0) {
                if (numitem < 1 || numitem % 8 != 0 && !window.isLoaded) {
                    $("#morebuttonContainer").hide();
                    setTimeout(function () {//needs to be delayed till rctop loaded
                        $('#rcTop').css('min-height', '0px');
                    }, 300);
                    //trackSiteDataFromJs("no_results");
                }
                else {
                    $("#morebuttonContainer").show();
                }
            }
        }
        catch (ex) {
            //Logger.debug(ex);
        }
    }

    function getItemsOnPage() {
        var numitem = 0;
        if ($("#resultscontainer").length > 0) {
            numitem = $("#resultscontainer").find('.galleryResContainer').length;
            if ($("#resultscontainer").find(".gitem").length > 0) {
                numitem += 2;
            }
        }
        return numitem;
    }

    function setCookie(cname, cvalue, exdays) {
        var d = new Date();
        d.setTime(d.getTime() + (exdays * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));
        var expires = "expires=" + d.toUTCString();
        document.cookie = cname + "=" + cvalue + "; " + expires;
    }

    function createCookie(name, value, days, decodeValue) {
        var expires;

        if (days && days !== null) {
            var date = new Date();
            date.setTime(date.getTime() + (days * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));
            expires = "; expires=" + date.toGMTString();
        } else {
            expires = "";
        }

        document.cookie = encodeURIComponent(name) + "=" + encodeURIComponent(value) + expires + "; path=/";
    }

    function readCookie(name) {
        var nameEQ = encodeURIComponent(name) + "=";
        var ca = document.cookie.split(';');
        for (var i = 0; i < ca.length; i++) {
            var c = ca[i];
            while (c.charAt(0) === ' ') c = c.substring(1, c.length);
            if (c.indexOf(nameEQ) === 0) return decodeURIComponent(c.substring(nameEQ.length, c.length));
        }
        return null;
    }

    function eraseCookie(name) {
        createCookie(name, "", -1);
    }

    function getParameterByName(name) {
        name = name.replace(/[\[]/, "\\\[").replace(/[\]]/, "\\\]");
        var regexString = "[\\?&]" + name + "=([^&#]*)";
        var regex = new RegExp(regexString);
        var found = regex.exec(window.location.search);
        if (found == null)
            return "";
        else
            return decodeURIComponent(found[1].replace(/\+/g, " "));
    }

    function removeFavoriteItem(element, event) {
        var favoriteItemDiv = $(element).closest(".favorite-item");

        favoriteItemDiv.fadeOut();
    }

    function ArrowBackToTopShowAndHide() {
        $(window).scroll(function () {
            $('.arrow_up_img').css('display', ($(this).scrollTop() > 500) ? 'block' : 'none');
        });
    }

    (function () {
        'use strict';

        $('.panel-trigger').on('mousemove', showPanel);
        $('.panel-trigger').on('mouseover', showPanel);
        $('.panel-trigger').on('mouseout', hidePanel);
        $('.panel-trigger > a').on('click', togglePanel);

        function togglePanel(e) {
            var obj = $(this).parent();
            obj.toggleClass('panel-visible');
        }

        function showPanel(e) {
            var obj = $(this);
            obj.addClass('panel-visible');
        }

        function hidePanel(e) {
            var obj = $(this);
            obj.removeClass('panel-visible');
        }
    })();

    $(document).on("click", ".mymarkIco", function (event) {

    function markIconClick(item) {
        var st = ($(item).attr('src'));
        var parent = item.parent();
        var uid = $(item).closest('.galleryResContainer').attr('data-uid');

        if (st.indexOf("_RO") > 1) { // remove mark
            parent.find(".mymarkIco").toggleClass('active');
            parent.find(".mymarkIco").removeAttr('style');
            var oldMarks = readCookie('Marks');
            var newMarks = oldMarks.replace(uid, '').replace('$$', '$');
            if (newMarks == "$") {
                newMarks = "";
            }
            eraseCookie("Marks");
            createCookie("Marks", newMarks);
        }

        else { //add mark
            debugger
            //pulse(parent.find(".mymarkIco.selected"));
            parent.find(".mymarkIco").toggleClass('active');
            parent.find(".mymarkIco").removeAttr('style');
            var oldMarks = readCookie('Marks');
            if (!oldMarks || oldMarks == "$") {
                oldMarks = "";
            }
            var newMarks = (oldMarks + "$" + uid).replace('$$', '$');
            eraseCookie("Marks");
            createCookie("Marks", newMarks);
        }
    }

    $("#footerNewsletterEmailButton").on('click', function () {
        var email = document.getElementById('footerNewsletterEmailInput').value;
        var re = /^([\w-]+(?:\.[\w-]+)*)@((?:[\w-]+\.)*\w[\w-]{0,66})\.([a-z]{2,6}(?:\.[a-z]{2})?)$/i;
        if (re.test(email)) {
            $("#footerNewsletterEmail").fadeOut(function () {
                $("#footerNewsletterConfirm").fadeIn();
            });
            $.post("/subscribe", {
                email: email
            });
            setCookie("newsl", "1", 365);
        }
        else {
            $("#footerNewsletterErr").show();
        }
    });

<script>
    (function (createCookie, eraseCookie, eventManager) {
        'use strict';
        // User Panel

        var wishlistSection = $('.user-panel .wishlist-section');
        var wishlistContainer = $('.user-panel .wishlist-content');
        var wishlistItems = $('.user-panel .wishlist-content .wishlist-item');
        var wishlistTitle = $('.user-panel .wishlist-section .title');
        var viewPrevious = $('.user-panel .view-previous');
        var viewNext = $('.user-panel .view-next');

        var pagingIndex = 0;
        var settings = {
            itemHeight: 165,
            titleMaxLength: 100,
            itemsPerPage: 1
        };


        var isScrolling = false;

        viewPrevious.on('click', viewPreviousClickHandler);
        viewNext.on('click', viewNextClickHandler);
        $('.user-panel').on('click', '.remove-button', removeButtonClickHandler);
        wishlistTitle.on('click', wishlistTitleClickHandler);



        init();

        function init() {
            updateNavigationArrows();
            if (!window.marks) {
                window.marks = new Marks(createCookie, eraseCookie);
            }
            loadWishlist();
            registerEventHandlers();
        }

        function registerEventHandlers() {
            $(window).on(eventManager.eventTypes.favoriteItemAdded, onFavoriteAdded);
            $(window).on(eventManager.eventTypes.favoriteItemRemoved, onFavoriteRemoved);
        }

        function onFavoriteAdded(e) {
            loadWishlist();
        }

        function onFavoriteRemoved(e) {
            loadWishlist();
        }

        function updateDom() {
            wishlistContainer = $('.user-panel .wishlist-content');
            wishlistItems = $('.user-panel .wishlist-content .wishlist-item');
        }

        function loadWishlist() {
            $.ajax({
                data: { container : 'user-panel' },
                url: '/getwishlist',
                method: 'GET',
                cache: false,
                success: function (response) {
                    if (response !== null) {
                        createWishlist(response);
                        updateNavigationArrows();
                    } else {
                        wishlistContainer.empty();
                    }
                },
                error: function (error) {
                    //Logger.debug('Could not load wishlist', error);
                }
            })
        }

        function createWishlist(list) {
            if (list == null) {
                return;

            }
            wishlistContainer.empty();
            for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
                createWishlistItem(list[i], i);
            }

            updateDom();
            updateNavigationArrows();
        }

        function createWishlistItem(item, index) {
            var element = $('<a/>');
            element.addClass('wishlist-item');
            if (item.source !== 'market') {
                element.attr('target', '_blank');
            }
            element.attr('title', item.title);

            if (item.source === 'MARKET_US_ELASTIC') {
                element.attr('href', '/p/' + item.id);
            }
            else {
                element.attr('href', item.redirectUrl);
            }

            var imgUrl = item.images[0];
            if (!imgUrl.toLowerCase().startsWith('http') && !imgUrl.toLowerCase().startsWith('"http'))
                imgUrl = item.images[0].url;

            var img = $('<img />');
            img.attr('src', imgUrl);
            img.appendTo(element);

            var productInfo = $('<div />');
            productInfo.addClass('product-info');

            var productTitle = $('<div />');
            productTitle.addClass('product-title');
            var title = item.title;
            if (item.title.length > settings.titleMaxLength) {
                title = title.substring(0, settings.titleMaxLength) + '<br />...';
            }
            productTitle.html(title);
            productTitle.appendTo(productInfo);

            var price = item.price.amount;

            var productPrice = $('<div />');
            productPrice.addClass('product-price');
            if (price <= -1) {
                productPrice.html('Price: TOO LOW');
            }
            else {
                productPrice.html('Price: $' + price.toFixed(2));
            }
            productPrice.appendTo(productInfo);

            var productStore = $('<div />');
            productStore.addClass('product-store');
            productStore.html(item.store);
            productStore.appendTo(productInfo);

            var removeButton = $('<span />');
            removeButton.addClass('stuccu-button white remove-button');
            removeButton.html('Remove');
            removeButton.attr('data-index', index);
            removeButton.attr('data-tracker-element', "");
            removeButton.attr('data-tracker-container', "Header");
            removeButton.attr('data-tracker-widget', "Wish list");
            removeButton.attr('data-tracker-action', "Remove Item");
            removeButton.attr('data-tracker-ga', "true");
            removeButton.attr('data-item-uid', getItemId(item));
            removeButton.appendTo(productInfo);

            productInfo.appendTo(element);

            var clear = $('<div />');
            clear.css('clear', 'both');
            clear.appendTo(element);

            element.appendTo(wishlistContainer);
        }

        function getItemId(item) {
            var source = item.source.toLowerCase()
            var uid = null;

            switch (source) {
                case 'market':
                    uid = item.source + '|' + item.sid;
                    break;
                case 'shopping':
                    uid = item.source + '|' + item.offerId;
                    break;
                case 'shopzilla':
                    if (item.sku !== undefined && item.sku !== null)
                        uid = item.source + '|' + item.sku + '*SKU*' + item.merchantId;
                    else if (item.upc !== undefined && item.upc !== null)
                        uid = item.source + '|' + item.upc + '*UPC*' + item.merchantId;
                    else if (item.szpid !== undefined && item.szpid !== null)
                        uid = item.source + '|' + item.sku + '*SZPID*' + item.merchantId;
                    else if (item.mpid !== undefined && item.mpid !== null)
                        uid = item.source + '|' + item.mpid + '*MPID*' + item.merchantId;
                    else if (item.szoid !== undefined && item.szoid !== null)
                        uid = item.source + '|' + item.sku + '*SZOID*' + item.merchantId;
                    break;
                default:
                    uid = item.source + '|' + item.id;
                    break;
            }

            return uid;
        }

        function viewPreviousClickHandler(e) {
            if ($(this).attr('disabled') === undefined && isScrolling === false) {
                scrollTo(pagingIndex, updateNavigationArrows, -1);
            }
        }

        function viewNextClickHandler(e) {
            if ($(this).attr('disabled') === undefined && isScrolling === false) {
                scrollTo(pagingIndex, updateNavigationArrows, 1);
            }
        }

        function removeButtonClickHandler(e) {

            e.preventDefault();
            var obj = $(this);
            var index = obj.data('index');
            var uid = obj.data('item-uid');
            marks.removeMark(uid);
            obj.closest('.wishlist-item').remove();

            updateDom();
            updateNavigationArrows();
        }

        function wishlistTitleClickHandler(e) {

            if (wishlistItems.size() === 0) {
                window.location.href = '/wishlist';
            } else {
                $('.user-panel .wishlist-section').toggleClass('content-visible');
            }

        }

        function scrollTo(itemIndex, callback, step) {
            isScrolling = true;
            pagingIndex = Math.ceil(itemIndex + step);
            var scrollPosition = pagingIndex * settings.itemHeight;

            wishlistContainer.animate({
                scrollTop: scrollPosition
            }, 200, 'swing', function () {
                callback();
                isScrolling = false;
            });
        }

        function updateNavigationArrows() {
            var itemsCount = wishlistItems.size();
            if (itemsCount > settings.itemsPerPage) {
                wishlistSection.removeClass('hidden-part');

                if (pagingIndex < 1) {
                    viewPrevious.attr('disabled', 'disabled');
                } else {
                    viewPrevious.removeAttr('disabled');
                }

                if (pagingIndex > itemsCount - 2) {
                    viewNext.attr('disabled', 'disabled');
                } else {
                    viewNext.removeAttr('disabled');
                }
            } else if (itemsCount > 0 && itemsCount <= settings.itemsPerPage) {
                wishlistSection.removeClass('hidden-part');
                viewPrevious.attr('disabled', 'disabled');
                viewNext.attr('disabled', 'disabled');
            } else {
                wishlistSection.addClass('hidden-part');
            }
        }
    })(createCookie, eraseCookie, eventManager);
</script>
<script>
    (function (cartManager, eventManager) {
        'use strict';
        // Cart Panel

        if (!eventManager)
            eventManager = new EventManager();

        if (!cartManager)
            cartManager = new CartManager(eventManager);

        var cartSummary = $('.cart-panel .summary');
        var cartItemsContainer = $('.cart-panel .cart-products-container');
        var cartProductsContainer = $('.cart-panel .cart-products-container .cart-products');
        var cartProducts = $('.cart-panel .cart-products-container .cart-products .product');
        var viewPrevious = $('.cart-panel .view-previous');
        var viewNext = $('.cart-panel .view-next');
        var footer = $('.cart-panel .footer');

        var totalItems = 0;

        var settings = {
            itemHeight: 144,
            itemsPerPage: 2,
            titleMaxLength: 14
        };

        var pagingIndex = 0;
        var isScrolling = false;

        viewPrevious.on('click', viewPreviousClickHandler);
        viewNext.on('click', viewNextClickHandler);
        $('.cart-panel').on('click', '.remove-button', removeButtonClickHandler);

        if (region == "en-US") {
            init();
        }
        

        function init() {
            updateNavigationArrows();
            if (!window.eventManager)
                window.eventManager = new EventManager();

            registerEventHandlers();
            loadCartItems();
        }

        function updateDom() {
            cartItemsContainer = $('.cart-panel .cart-products-container');
            cartProductsContainer = $('.cart-panel .cart-products-container .cart-products');
            cartProducts = $('.cart-panel .cart-products-container .cart-products .product');
        }

        function registerEventHandlers() {
            $(window).on(eventManager.eventTypes.cartItemAdded, onCartItemAdded);
            $(window).on(eventManager.eventTypes.cartItemUpdated, onCartItemUpdated);
            $(window).on(eventManager.eventTypes.cartItemRemoved, onCartItemRemoved);
        }

        function loadCartItems() {
            $.ajax({
                url: '/cart/getcartitems',
                method: 'GET',
                cache: false,
            }).done(function (response) {
                if (response !== null && response !== undefined && response.items !=null) {
                    var data = response;
                    createCartItems(data.items);
                    setCartSummary(totalItems, data.totalAmount);
                    setActionButtons(totalItems);
                } else {
                    setCartSummary(0, 0);
                    setActionButtons(0);
                }
            }).fail(function (err) { // If request failed, show empty cart panel
                setCartSummary(0, 0);
                setActionButtons(0);
            });
        }

        function viewPreviousClickHandler(e) {
            if ($(this).attr('disabled') === undefined && isScrolling === false) {
                scrollTo(pagingIndex, updateNavigationArrows, -1);
            }
        }

        function viewNextClickHandler(e) {
            if ($(this).attr('disabled') === undefined && isScrolling === false) {
                scrollTo(pagingIndex, updateNavigationArrows, 1);
            }
        }

        function removeButtonClickHandler(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            var obj = $(this);
            var sid = obj.data('item-uid');

            cartManager.updateCart(sid, cartManager.cartActions.remove)
                .success(function (response) { });
        }

        function setCartSummary(itemCount, sum) {
            if (itemCount > 0) {
                cartSummary.html('Total ' + itemCount + ' items: $' + sum.toFixed(2));
                $('.cart-panel').parent().find('.navbartext').html('Cart (' + itemCount + ')');
            } else {
                cartSummary.html('Hmm... Your shopping cart is empty');
                $('.cart-panel').parent().find('.navbartext').html('Cart');
                $('.cart-panel .footer').hide(); //remeber to remove when we get the new portal page 
            }
        }

        function setActionButtons(productCount) {
            var hasProducts = productCount > 0;
            if (hasProducts) {
                footer.removeClass('no-products');
            } else {
                footer.addClass('no-products');
            }
        }

        function createCartItems(list) {
            cartProductsContainer.empty();
            totalItems = 0;
            for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
                createCartItem(list[i]);
            }

            updateDom();
            updateNavigationArrows();
        }

        function createCartItem(item) {
            var element = $('<a/>');
            element.addClass('product');
            element.attr('title', item.title);
            element.attr('href', '/p/' + item.sid);
            var img = $('<img />');
            img.attr('src', getCurrentProtocolUrl(item.image));
            img.appendTo(element);

            var productInfo = $('<div />');
            productInfo.addClass('product-info');

            var productTitle = $('<div />');
            productTitle.addClass('product-title');
            var title = item.title;
            if (title.length > settings.titleMaxLength - 3) {
                title = title.substring(0, settings.titleMaxLength - 3) + '...';
            }
            productTitle.html(title);
            productTitle.appendTo(productInfo);

            var productQuantity = $('<div />');
            productQuantity.addClass('product-quantity');
            productQuantity.html('Qty: ' + item.neededQuantity);
            productQuantity.appendTo(productInfo);

            var productPrice = $('<div />');
            productPrice.addClass('product-price');
            productPrice.html('Price: $' + item.totalPrice);
            productPrice.appendTo(productInfo);

            var removeButton = $('<span />');
            removeButton.addClass('stuccu-button white remove-button');
            removeButton.html('Remove');
            removeButton.attr('data-item-uid', item.sid);
            element.attr('data-tracker-element', "");
            element.attr('data-tracker-container', "Header");
            element.attr('data-tracker-widget', "Cart");
            element.attr('data-tracker-action', "Remove Item");
            element.attr('data-tracker-ga', "true");
            removeButton.appendTo(productInfo);

            productInfo.appendTo(element);

            var clear = $('<div />');
            clear.css('clear', 'both');
            clear.appendTo(element);

            element.appendTo(cartProductsContainer);

            totalItems += item.neededQuantity;
        }

        function getCurrentProtocolUrl(url) {
            var currentProtocol = window.location.protocol;
            var idx = url.toLowerCase().indexOf('http://');
            if (idx >= 0) {
                return url.substring(5);
            }

            return url;
        }

        function onCartItemAdded(e) {
            loadCartItems();
        }

        function onCartItemUpdated(e) {
            loadCartItems();
        }

        function onCartItemRemoved(e) {
            loadCartItems();
        }

        function scrollTo(itemIndex, callback, step) {
            isScrolling = true;
            pagingIndex = Math.ceil(itemIndex + step);
            var scrollPosition = pagingIndex * settings.itemHeight;

            cartProductsContainer.animate({
                scrollTop: scrollPosition
            }, 200, 'swing', function () {
                callback();
                isScrolling = false;
            });
        }

        function updateNavigationArrows() {
            var itemsCount = cartProducts.size();
            if (itemsCount > settings.itemsPerPage) {
                cartItemsContainer.removeClass('hidden-part');

                if (pagingIndex < 1) {
                    viewPrevious.attr('disabled', 'disabled');
                } else {
                    viewPrevious.removeAttr('disabled');
                }

                if (pagingIndex > itemsCount - 3) {
                    viewNext.attr('disabled', 'disabled');
                } else {
                    viewNext.removeAttr('disabled');
                }
            } else if (itemsCount > 0 && itemsCount <= settings.itemsPerPage) {
                cartItemsContainer.removeClass('hidden-part');
                viewPrevious.attr('disabled', 'disabled');
                viewNext.attr('disabled', 'disabled');
            } else {
                cartItemsContainer.addClass('hidden-part');
            }
        }
    })(cartManager, eventManager);
</script>
<script>
    (function (cartManager, eventManager, marks) {
        'use strict';
        // Single Product Page

        if (!eventManager)
            eventManager = new EventManager();

        if (!cartManager)
            cartManager = new CartManager(eventManager);

        if (!marks)
            marks = new Marks(window.createCookie, window.eraseCookie, eventManager);

        var errorCodes = {
            invalidQuantity: 1,
            itemAlreadyInCart: 2,
            maximumQuantityInCart: 3
        };

        var imageContainer = $('.single-product .product-photo');
        var imageElement = imageContainer.find('img');
        var currentThumbnailIndex = 0;
        var thumbnailElements = $('.thumbnails-container .thumb');
        var sid = $('.single-product').data('sid');
        var markIcon = $('.single-product .item-data-top .add-to-favorites .mark-icon');
        var thumbScrollButtonsContainer = $('thumb-scroll-icons');

        $('.single-product').on('click', '.thumbnails-container .thumb', thumbnailClickHandler);
        $('.single-product .addToCart-button').on('click', addToCartClickHandler);
        $('.single-product .goToCart-button').on('click', goToCartClickHandler);
        $('.single-product .buy-button').on('click', buyNowClickHandler);
        //$('[data-load-url]').on('click', loadUrlClickHandler);
        $('.thumb-scroll-icons .left-arrow').on('click', previousThumbnail);
        $('.thumb-scroll-icons .right-arrow').on('click', nextThumbnail);
        $('.single-product .add-to-favorites').on('click', addToFavoritesClickHandler);

        init();

        function init() {
            hideErrors();
            checkCartItems();
            $(window).on(eventManager.eventTypes.favoriteItemAdded, setMarkIcon);
            $(window).on(eventManager.eventTypes.favoriteItemRemoved, removeMarkIcon);

            var markId = 'MARKET_US_ELASTIC|' + sid;
            if (marks.isMarked(markId)) {
                setMarkIcon();
            } else {
                removeMarkIcon();
            }

            if (thumbnailElements.size() <= 1) {
                thumbScrollButtonsContainer.hide();
            } else {
                thumbScrollButtonsContainer.show();
            }
        }

        function checkCartItems() {
            var count = parseInt($('.single-product .addToCart-button').data('cart-item-count'));
            if (count > 0) {
                showError('cart', errorCodes.itemAlreadyInCart);
            }
            if ($(".single-product .goToCart-button").length > 0) {
                showError("cart", errorCodes.maximumQuantityInCart);
            }
        }

        function thumbnailClickHandler(e) {
            var obj = $(this);
            currentThumbnailIndex = obj.data('index');
            var src = obj.find('img').attr('src');
            setMainImage(src);
        }

        function addToCartClickHandler(e) {
            hideErrors();
            var obj = $(this);
            var title = obj.data('title');
            addItemToCart(obj, function (response) {
                cartManager.submitCartUpdate(title);
            });
        }

        function goToCartClickHandler(e) {
            location.href = "/cart";
        }

        function buyNowClickHandler(e) {
            var obj = $(this);
            var isAuthenticated = obj.data("authenticated");

            addItemToCart(obj, function (response) {
                if (isAuthenticated === true) {
                    window.location.href = '/checkout/begin';
                } else {
                    window.location.href = '/account?returnUrl=/checkout/begin';
                }
            });
        }

        function loadUrlClickHandler(e) {
            var obj = $(this);
            var contentLoaded = obj.data('content-loaded');
            if (contentLoaded)
                return;

            var url = obj.data('load-url');
            var containerId = obj.data('load-container-id');
            $.ajax({
                method: 'GET',
                url: url,
                success: function (response) {
                    $('#' + containerId).html(response.trim());
                    obj.data('content-loaded', true);
                }
            });
        }

        function previousThumbnail(e) {
            if (currentThumbnailIndex === 0)
                currentThumbnailIndex = thumbnailElements.size();

            currentThumbnailIndex--;

            var thumbElement = $(thumbnailElements[currentThumbnailIndex]);
            var src = thumbElement.children().attr('src')
            setMainImage(src);
        }

        function nextThumbnail(e) {
            currentThumbnailIndex++;

            if (currentThumbnailIndex === thumbnailElements.size())
                currentThumbnailIndex = 0;

            var thumbElement = $(thumbnailElements[currentThumbnailIndex]);
            var src = thumbElement.children().attr('src')
            setMainImage(src);
        }

        function addToFavoritesClickHandler(e) {
            var markId = 'MARKET_US_ELASTIC|' + sid;
            if (marks.isMarked(markId)) {
                marks.removeMark(markId);
            } else {
                marks.addMark(markId);
            }
        }

        function setMarkIcon(e, data) {
            markIcon.removeClass('not-selected');
        }

        function removeMarkIcon(e, data) {
            if (common.isDefined(e) && common.isDefined(data)) {
                if (sid === data.itemId.substring(data.itemId.indexOf('|') + 1)) {
                    markIcon.addClass('not-selected');
                }
            }
        }

        function setMainImage(src) {
            imageElement.attr('src', src);
        }

        function addItemToCart(obj, successCallback) {

            var sid = obj.data('sid');
            var stockCount = parseInt(obj.data('stock'));
            var cartItemCount = parseInt(obj.data('cart-item-count'));
            var addQuantity = parseInt($('.single-product .order-quantity').attr('value'));

            if (cartManager.validateBeforeAdd(stockCount, cartItemCount, addQuantity)) {
                var promise = cartManager.updateCart(sid, 1, addQuantity);
                promise.success(function (response) {
                    if (successCallback !== undefined && successCallback !== null) {
                        if (response.neededQuantity === response.finalQuantity) {
                            successCallback(response);
                        } else {
                            showError('cart', errorCodes.invalidQuantity);
                        }
                    }
                });
            } else {
                showError('cart', errorCodes.invalidQuantity);
            }
        }

        function showError(container, errorCode) {
            var containerElement = $('.single-product [data-error-container=' + container + ']');
            var errorElement = containerElement.find('span[data-error-code=' + errorCode + ']');
            errorElement.show();
            containerElement.addClass('has-error');
        }

        function hideErrors() {
            var containers = ['cart'];
            for (var i = 0; i < containers.length; i++) {
                var containerElement = $('.single-product [data-error-container=' + containers[i] + ']');
                var errorElement = containerElement.find('span');
                errorElement.hide();
                containerElement.removeClass('has-error');
            }
        }
    })(cartManager, eventManager);
</script>
<script>
    (function () {
        'use strict';
        // Custom Drop Down
        var selector = '.stuccu-dropdown';

        $(document).ready(function () {
            addDropDownStyle();
        });

        $(function () {
            $(document).click(function () {
                // all dropdowns
                $('.dropdown-wrapper').removeClass('active');
            });
        });

        function addDropDownStyle() {
            $(selector).each(function (idx, element) {
                createDropDownMarkup(element);
            });
        }

        function createDropDownMarkup(selectElement) {
            var element = $(selectElement);

            var wrapper = $('<div />');
            wrapper.attr('tabindex', '0');

            var attributes = selectElement.attributes;
            for (var i = attributes.length - 1; i >= 0 ; i--) {
                var attrName = attributes[i].name;
                var attrValue = attributes[i].value;
                if (attributes[i].name.toLowerCase() === 'class') {
                    var selectorName = selector.replace('.', '');
                    attrValue = attrValue.replace(selectorName, '');
                }

                wrapper.attr(attrName, attrValue);
                selectElement.attributes.removeNamedItem(attrName);
            }

            wrapper.addClass('dropdown-wrapper');
            wrapper.on('click', function (e) { $(this).toggleClass('active'); return false; });

            var placeholder = element.attr('placeholder');
            var placeholderElement = $('<span />');
            var hasPlaceholder = false;
            if (placeholder !== undefined && placeholder !== null && placeholder.trim() !== '') {
                placeholderElement.html(placeholder);
                hasPlaceholder = true;
            }

            placeholderElement.appendTo(wrapper);

            var dropdownList = $('<ul />');
            dropdownList.addClass('dropdown-list');

            for (var i = 0; i < selectElement.options.length; i++) {
                var li = $('<li />');
                var text = $('<span />');
                text.attr('data-value', selectElement.options[i].value);
                text.html(selectElement.options[i].text);
                text.appendTo(li);

                text.on('click', function (e) {
                    placeholderElement.html($(this).data('value'));
                    wrapper.attr('value', $(this).data('value'));
                });

                li.appendTo(dropdownList);
            }

            if (!hasPlaceholder && dropdownList.children().size() > 0) {
                var firstItemValue = $(dropdownList.find('span')[0]).data('value');
                placeholderElement.html(firstItemValue);
                wrapper.attr('value', firstItemValue);
            }

            dropdownList.appendTo(wrapper);

            element.parent().append(wrapper);
            element.hide();
        }
    })();
</script>
<script>
            !function ($) {

                "use strict";

                var Typed = function (el, options) {

                    // chosen element to manipulate text
                    this.el = $(el);
                    // options
                    this.options = $.extend({}, $.fn.typed.defaults, options);

                    // text content of element
                    this.text = this.el.text();

                    // typing speed
                    this.typeSpeed = this.options.typeSpeed;

                    // add a delay before typing starts
                    this.startDelay = this.options.startDelay;

                    // backspacing speed
                    this.backSpeed = this.options.backSpeed;

                    // amount of time to wait before backspacing
                    this.backDelay = this.options.backDelay;

                    // input strings of text
                    this.strings = this.options.strings;

                    // character number position of current string
                    this.strPos = 0;

                    // current array position
                    this.arrayPos = 0;

                    // number to stop backspacing on.
                    // default 0, can change depending on how many chars
                    // you want to remove at the time
                    this.stopNum = 0;

                    // Looping logic
                    this.loop = this.options.loop;
                    this.loopCount = this.options.loopCount;
                    this.curLoop = 0;

                    // for stopping
                    this.stop = false;

                    // All systems go!
                    this.build();
                };

                Typed.prototype = {

                    constructor: Typed

                    , init: function () {
                        // begin the loop w/ first current string (global self.string)
                        // current string will be passed as an argument each time after this
                        var self = this;
                        self.timeout = setTimeout(function () {
                            // Start typing
                            self.typewrite(self.strings[self.arrayPos], self.strPos);
                        }, self.startDelay);
                    }

                    , build: function () {
                        // Insert cursor
                        this.cursor = $("<span class=\"typed-cursor\">|</span>");
                        this.el.after(this.cursor);
                        this.init();
                    }

                    // pass current string state to each function, types 1 char per call
                    , typewrite: function (curString, curStrPos) {
                        // exit when stopped
                        if (this.stop === true)
                            return;

                        // varying values for setTimeout during typing
                        // can't be global since number changes each time loop is executed
                        var humanize = Math.round(Math.random() * (100 - 30)) + this.typeSpeed;
                        var self = this;

                        // contain typing function in a timeout humanize'd delay
                        self.timeout = setTimeout(function () {
                            // check for an escape character before a pause value
                            // format: \^\d+ eg: ^1000 should be able to print the ^ too using ^^
                            // single ^ are removed from string
                            var charPause = 0;
                            var substr = curString.substr(curStrPos);
                            if (substr.charAt(0) === '^') {
                                var e = 1;
                                if (substr.match(/^\^\d+/)) {
                                    substr = substr.replace(/^\^(\d+).*/, "$1");
                                    e += substr.length;
                                    charPause = parseInt(substr);
                                }

                                // strip out the escape character and pause value so they're not printed
                                curString = curString.substring(0, curStrPos) + curString.substring(curStrPos + e);
                            }

                            // timeout for any pause after a character
                            self.timeout = setTimeout(function () {
                                if (curStrPos === curString.length) {
                                    // fires callback function
                                    self.options.onStringTyped(self.arrayPos);

                                    // is this the final string
                                    if (self.arrayPos === self.strings.length - 1) {
                                        // animation that occurs on the last typed string
                                        self.options.callback();

                                        self.curLoop++;

                                        // quit if we wont loop back
                                        if (self.loop === false || self.curLoop === self.loopCount)
                                            return;
                                    }

                                    self.timeout = setTimeout(function () {
                                        self.backspace(curString, curStrPos);
                                    }, self.backDelay);
                                } else {

                                    /* call before functions if applicable */
                                    if (curStrPos === 0)
                                        self.options.preStringTyped(self.arrayPos);

                                    // start typing each new char into existing string
                                    // curString: arg, self.text: original text inside element
                                    self.el.text(self.text + curString.substr(0, curStrPos + 1));

                                    // add characters one by one
                                    curStrPos++;
                                    // loop the function
                                    self.typewrite(curString, curStrPos);
                                }
                                // end of character pause
                            }, charPause);

                            // humanized value for typing
                        }, humanize);

                    }

                    , backspace: function (curString, curStrPos) {
                        // exit when stopped
                        if (this.stop === true) {
                            return;
                        }

                        // varying values for setTimeout during typing
                        // can't be global since number changes each time loop is executed
                        var humanize = Math.round(Math.random() * (100 - 30)) + this.backSpeed;
                        var self = this;

                        self.timeout = setTimeout(function () {

                            // ----- this part is optional ----- //
                            // check string array position
                            // on the first string, only delete one word
                            // the stopNum actually represents the amount of chars to
                            // keep in the current string. In my case it's 14.
                            // if (self.arrayPos == 1){
                            //  self.stopNum = 14;
                            // }
                            //every other time, delete the whole typed string
                            // else{
                            //  self.stopNum = 0;
                            // }

                            // ----- continue important stuff ----- //
                            // replace text with current text + typed characters
                            self.el.text(self.text + curString.substr(0, curStrPos));

                            // if the number (id of character in current string) is
                            // less than the stop number, keep going
                            if (curStrPos > self.stopNum) {
                                // subtract characters one by one
                                curStrPos--;
                                // loop the function
                                self.backspace(curString, curStrPos);
                            }
                                // if the stop number has been reached, increase
                                // array position to next string
                            else if (curStrPos <= self.stopNum) {
                                self.arrayPos++;

                                if (self.arrayPos === self.strings.length) {
                                    self.arrayPos = 0;
                                    self.init();
                                } else
                                    self.typewrite(self.strings[self.arrayPos], curStrPos);
                            }

                            // humanized value for typing
                        }, humanize);

                    }

                    // Start & Stop currently not working

                    // , stop: function() {
                    //     var self = this;

                    //     self.stop = true;
                    //     clearInterval(self.timeout);
                    // }

                    // , start: function() {
                    //     var self = this;
                    //     if(self.stop === false)
                    //        return;

                    //     this.stop = false;
                    //     this.init();
                    // }

                    // Reset and rebuild the element
                    , reset: function () {
                        var self = this;
                        clearInterval(self.timeout);
                        var id = this.el.attr('id');
                        this.el.after('<span id="' + id + '" />')
                        this.el.remove();
                        this.cursor.remove();
                        // Send the callback
                        self.options.resetCallback();
                    }

                };

                $.fn.typed = function (option) {
                    return this.each(function () {
                        var $this = $(this)
                          , data = $this.data('typed')
                          , options = typeof option == 'object' && option;
                        if (!data) $this.data('typed', (data = new Typed(this, options)));
                        if (typeof option == 'string') data[option]();
                    });
                };

                $.fn.typed.defaults = {
                    strings: ["These are the default values...", "You know what you should do?", "Use your own!", "Have a great day!"],
                    // typing speed
                    typeSpeed: 0,
                    // time before typing starts
                    startDelay: 0,
                    // backspacing speed
                    backSpeed: 0,
                    // time before backspacing
                    backDelay: 500,
                    // loop
                    loop: false,
                    // false = infinite
                    loopCount: false,
                    // call when done callback function
                    callback: function () { },
                    // starting callback function before each string
                    preStringTyped: function () { },
                    //callback for every typed string
                    onStringTyped: function () { },
                    // callback for reset
                    resetCallback: function () { }
                };


            }(window.jQuery);
    </script>
<script>
            //Home page script for typing effect and img carousel

            var strings = ["Fitbit Surge", "iPhone 6s", "Pie Face Game", "PlayStation 4"];
            var images = ["fitbit.jpg", "iphone66.jpg", "pie-face.jpg", "playstayson.jpg"];
            var i = 0;

            $(function () {
                $(".typedTxt").typed({
                    strings: ["Fitbit Surge", "iPhone 6s", "Pie Face Game", "PlayStation 4"],
                    typeSpeed: 50,
                    backDelay: 3000,
                    backSpeed: 10,
                    loop: true,
                    showCursor: true,
                    contentType: 'html',
                    onStringTyped: function () {
                        setTimeout(function () {
                            changeImg();
                            i++;

                            if (i > images.length - 1) {
                                i = 0;
                            }
                        }, 3000);
                    }
                });
            });

            $(".typedTxt").click(function () {
                if (i < 3) {
                    window.location.href = window.location + "s/" + strings[i];
                }
                else {
                    window.location.href = window.location + "s/" + strings[i - 1];
                }

            });

            function changeImg() {
                var div = $("#homepage_banner");
                div.fadeTo(800, 0.1, function () {
                    $(this).css('background-image', 'url(//static.stuccu.com/images/designs/home_page/hero/' + images[i] + ')');
                }).fadeTo(600, 1);

            }


    </script>
<script>
    (function () {

        // Indicates whether or not the tabs are fixed to the top of the page.
        var _isFixedToTop = false;

        // Should add pixels when checking whether or not to fix the tabs to top on scroll - if homepage upper categories bar is visible.
        var _pixelAddition = getPixelAddition();

        $(document).ready(function () {
            window.pageName = "HomePage";

            $(document).on("scroll ready", function () {
                fixDivsToTop();
            });

            $(window).resize(function () {
                _pixelAddition = getPixelAddition();
            });

            // Clicking on magnifying glass image submit search.
            $("#homepage_search_bar_magnifying_glass").click(function (e) {
                submitSearch("#homepage_search_bar_input");
            });

            // Linking through javascript to overcome style problems.
            $(".homepage_upper_category").click(function (e) {

                // Only if user clicked with left mouse button.
                if (e.originalEvent.button == "0") {
                    window.location.href = $(this).data("link");
                }
            });

            // Logic for tabs.
            $(".homepage_nav").click(function () {
                if ($(this).hasClass("disabled")) {
                    return;
                }
                $("#homepage").removeClass("explore-active coupons-active products-active articles-active favorites-active");
                var prefix = $(this).data("prefix");
                $("#homepage").addClass(prefix + "-active");
            });

            // Heart color logic.
            $(".homepage_tile_favorite").mousedown(function () {
                var favImg = $(this).find(".homepage_tile_favorite_img").first();
                favImg.toggleClass("pressed");
                if (favImg.hasClass("pressed")) {
                    favImg.addClass("justClicked");
                }
            }).mouseleave(function () {
                var favImg = $(this).find(".homepage_tile_favorite_img").first();
                favImg.removeClass("justClicked");
            });

            // Linking tags on tiles to search page.
            $(".homepage_tile_tag").click(function (e) {
                e.preventDefault();
                location.href = '/s/' + $(this).data("tag");
            });

            // Logic for opening and coloring the upper categories bar.
            $(".homepage_upper_category").hover(function () {
                $(this).addClass("hover");
            }, function () {
                $(".homepage_upper_category").removeClass("hover");
            });
            $(".homepage_upper_categories_expanded, .homepage_upper_category_name").hover(function () {
                $(this).closest(".homepage_upper_category").addClass("hover");
            }, function () {
                $(".homepage_upper_category").removeClass("hover");
            });
        });

        function fixDivsToTop() {
            var scrollPosition = $(document).scrollTop();

            if (!_isFixedToTop && scrollPosition >= 350 + _pixelAddition) {
                $("#homepage_tiles_upper_text_container").addClass("fixed");
                $("#homepage_middle_navbar").addClass("fixed");
                $("#homepage_tiles").addClass("fixed");
                _isFixedToTop = true;
            }
            else if (_isFixedToTop && scrollPosition < 350 + _pixelAddition) {
                $("#homepage_tiles_upper_text_container").removeClass("fixed");
                $("#homepage_middle_navbar").removeClass("fixed");
                $("#homepage_tiles").removeClass("fixed");
                _isFixedToTop = false;
            }
        }

        function getPixelAddition() {
            var pixelAddition = 0;

            var upperCategoriesBar = $("#homepage_upper_categories_bar");

            if (upperCategoriesBar.length < 0 && upperCategoriesBar.css("display") != "none") {
                pixelAddition += upperCategoriesBar.height();
            }

            return pixelAddition;
        }
    }());

    fbq('track', 'ViewContent');
    </script>
